Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the martingales structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 595. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 793 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).
